2026-06-19 12:04:08.467 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (32ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

2026-06-19 12:06:03.089 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (9ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]
2026-06-19 12:06:03.091 +03:00 [INF] Executing ObjectResult, writing value of type 'TicketingSystem.API.Common.ApiResponse'.
2026-06-19 12:06:03.091 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (11ms) [Parameters=[@p='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'

FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]

SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]
2026-06-19 12:24:39.219 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (12ms) [Parameters=[@p='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT TOP(@p) [u].[FullName] AS [EmployeeName], COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) AS [ResolvedCount]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] = [u].[Id]
WHERE [t].[AssignedEmployeeId] IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC

GROUP BY [t].[AssignedEmployeeId], [u].[FullName]
ORDER BY COUNT(CASE
    WHEN [t].[Status] = 3 THEN 1
END) DESC
2026-06-19 12:25:12.421 +03:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (0ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT CASE [t].[Status]
    WHEN 0 THEN N'New'
    WHEN 1 THEN N'Assigned'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'InProgress'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Resolved'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'Closed'
    ELSE CAST([t].[Status] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [Status], COUNT(*) AS [Count]
FROM [Tickets] AS [t]
GROUP BY [t].[Status]
